# Manuals Reference Index

Generated from the manuals-split task (mapping.json, sections.json, staging index.json files) and a scan of every `reference` folder under OM9_Milestones. This is a new file; no L1.json/L2.json or other existing file was modified.

Each function folder listed below has a `reference/` subfolder with `REFERENCE.md` (entry point), `text/` (section excerpts as .md) and `images/` (screenshots as .png). Follow the REFERENCE.md link in each row.
